Feeder FIFO physical mapping qualification - 2026-10-31

Previous goal turn: progress. Signed root 23377cc/docs 8651481 committed the
complete tested transport milestone; combined timing remained 4/9 at 100MHz.
All five failing critical paths went from payload BRAM to distributed FIFO
storage, with 5.8..6.1ns logic and approximately 4.1..4.3ns routing.

Requirements: WSE-STD-BRAM-RAM sections 2/3.1/5.3/6/8/11 and current stream/
window profiles. The existing FIFO remains exactly two 128-bit words (32B),
with unchanged pointers, reservations, write/read edges and latency. The
only functional-source difference is ram_style=logic; comments and that
attribute removed, the complete RTL token stream equals the committed one.
All other six RTL dependencies are byte-identical. N64, K2/4/8, int16 stored
coefficients, phase precision, headroom, RNE, saturation and payload-bank
geometry remain fixed. No hardware deadline or test threshold is relaxed.

The FIFO trades 32 distributed-RAM cells (256 physical bytes, of which only
32 logical bytes were used) for 256 FFs. LUT/mux changes are measured below.
No payload BRAM or DSP count changes. This is one shared feeder, not a claim
that this whole top should be replicated per voice. Aggregate packing,
clock load and board power remain unqualified.

An isolated K8/seed3 probe improved 98.85330 to 102.85949MHz. It did not change
production aliases. The final complete matrices below use current source
hashes and verified ABC9 mapping; every failed placement is retained.

tools/check_hdl_fifo.py extracts the actual three FIFO declarations/accesses,
rejects changed widths or additional accesses, maps this isolated storage to
ECP5, and proves the complete one-clock transition plus asynchronous read
for every binary prior state, payload, write/read pointer, reset and enable.
All 256 mapped FF outputs are cut at arbitrary shared prior contents; no
reset, initialization or reachability assumption is used. The accepted FF
configuration has positive clock, CE and inactive set/reset. Other FF
configurations fail closed. LUT/mux behavior is read from the installed
cells_sim.v; the flattened proof netlist and hash retain that model.
This proves the isolated mapped FIFO, not payload DP16KD, full stream,
clock/reset physical behavior or a routed/device netlist. It makes no claim
about unread/uninitialized data. Integration verifies valid publication,
reservations and exact audio after writes. Two actual mapped-netlist mutants
(corrupt write data and corrupt read output) must fail without a PASS report.

Current modified-path runs: 20 RTL simulations, 47 formal/evidence tests,
and 4 mapped-FIFO tests. The 41 unchanged owner/burst/priority/bounds tests
retain matching sources and benches from the preceding milestone. Total
112 distinct tests through scoped runs; no new whole-root run is claimed.
Audio comparisons cover 141824 stream and 21504 windowed samples, including
stalls, exact shared deadlines, reset and fallback. Ready latency stays
331/331/642 clocks and fallback18, under unchanged assertions.

Hardware budgets remain 100MHz, 40000 shared DMA clocks and 133333 audio
clocks. Full voice/operator generation, admission, global memory packing,
I/O/global clocks, PHY/board integration, Mode A storage and K2 tier cost
remain open. Out-of-context component timing cannot close those gates.

Reproduce: pixi run check-hdl-fifo; check-hdl-ccs-stream; check-hdl-dma-window;
the corresponding synth-hdl/route-hdl tasks; tests/conformance/test_hdl_fifo.py.
